39	| 28 | `check_agent` | core.check | Agent, Env → Lineup | a lone agent on a one-sided environment becomes `{side: agent}`; each side's agent's `kind` against its side's moves; a side with no agent raises `MissingSideError` | CORE-07, CORE-10 |
40	| 29 | `training_seeds` | evaluate.seeds | run seed, n games → Seeds | generator from the run seed over `[0, 1_000_000)` (AD-04) | EVAL-01, QR-05 |
41	| 30 | `create_run` | runs.store | RunConfig → RunWriter | new `root/<env>/<name>/` folder, config written at once; refuses an existing name (AD-15) | LOG-01 |
42	| 31 | `JsonRunWriter` | runs.writer | RunConfig, path → RunWriter | record in memory, atomic rewrite on `add_eval`, `save_generation` and `finish` (with `final` per side); `save_agent` and `save_generation` write `torch.save` files at `agent_path` (AD-05) | LOG-02–LOG-05, SELF-02 |
43	| 32 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | `run/<side>.pt`, or `run/generations/<side>-<NN>.pt` | LOG-04, SELF-02 |
44	| 33 | `evaluate` | evaluate.score | Env, Lineup, seeds, side → EvalResult | `check_agent`, then `play_game` greedily on each seed; `side`'s scores, mean and std | EVAL-02, AGENT-03, PG-02 |

48	```python
49	def run_train(args: Namespace) -> RunRecord:
50	    config = train_config(args)
51	    setup = make_run(config)
52	    return train_run(setup.env, setup.learners, config, opponents=setup.opponents, schedule=setup.schedule, root=args.root)

55	## PL-02: Score the baselines

57	**Purpose:** Score the random and scripted agents of an environment, for each side, on the same 100 held-out games as its learners, so their curves have bars to clear.
58	**Covers:** BASE-01–BASE-06, EVAL-01, REPLAY-01

63	| 2 | `score_baselines` | study.baselines | Env, root → RunRecord (one per baseline) | one-sided: `baseline-random` and `baseline-scripted`; two-sided: `baseline-random-<side>` per side (that side random, every other scripted) and `baseline-scripted` (every side scripted) (AD-24); per baseline an existing finished record is read back with `load_record`; otherwise create it under the environment's folder, `evaluate` each side on `EVAL_SEEDS`, `add_eval`, `finish` with those results as `final` | BASE-03, EVAL-01 |

67	| 6 | `ScriptedFish` | baselines.fish | name → Agent | from the rays only: speed 0.3; the wall within 1 unit ahead → turn 15° toward more room; else away from the ray seeing the ball closest; else straight | BASE-04 |
68	| 7 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.predator | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | BASE-05 |

83	**Entry:** `src/rllab/curves/pipeline.py::run_curves` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab curves runs/tank/ppo-s1 runs/tank/no-cap-s1 [--side prey] --out chart.png`
84	**Purpose:** Redraw learning curves from saved run records, one side per chart, with that side's baselines as flat lines, without training again.
85	**Covers:** CURVE-01–CURVE-07, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, TWIN-02, QR-04

87	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
88	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
89	| 1 | `load_records` | curves.inputs | run paths → RunRecord (one per path) | `load_record` per path; skips and names a record with no evaluation points | CURVE-05 |
90	| 2 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | CURVE-05 |
91	| 3 | `find_baselines` | curves.inputs | root, RunRecord → RunRecord (each baseline found) | `root/<env>/baseline-*` records of the records' environment, so compass-tank runs get the tank's and runs of any fish or prey count get the default bars | CURVE-03, TWIN-02 |
92	| 4 | `plot_curves` | curves.plot | RunRecord, side → CurvePlot | Matplotlib Agg PNG of `side`'s evaluation results (the only side when none is given): one mean line with a min–max band per config minus seed, labelled `full` or the DQN's switches off, a rung's name plus any switch off, a self-play rung's name, `compass` added; each baseline whose other sides play `scripted` as a flat line (AD-07) | CURVE-01–CURVE-07, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, QR-04 |
93	
94	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
95	```python
96	def run_curves(args: Namespace) -> CurvePlot:
97	    records = load_records(args.runs)
98	    baselines = find_baselines(args.root, records)
99	    return plot_curves(records, args.out, baselines=baselines, side=args.side)
100	```

135	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
136	```python
137	def run_study(env_name="snake", seeds=(1, 2, 3, 4, 5), *, n_games=1_000, workers=None, skip=(), root=Path("runs")) -> StudyReport:
138	    plan = study_plan(env_name)
139	    configs = study_configs(plan, seeds, n_games, skip=skip)
140	    records = train_variants(configs, root, workers=workers)
141	    tables = play_tables(plan, records, root, workers=workers)
142	    env = make_env(plan.env, **dict(plan.env_options))
143	    baselines = score_baselines(env, root=root)
144	    comparisons = compare_runs(plan, records, baselines, tables)
145	    charts = draw_charts(plan, records, baselines, root / plan.env / "study")
146	    table_files = write_tables(plan, tables, root / plan.env / "study")
147	    return write_report(plan, comparisons, charts, table_files, records, root)
148	```

150	## PL-05: Watch a replay
151	**Entry:** `src/rllab/replay/pipeline.py::run_replay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab replay runs/tank/ppo-s1 --env tank --seed 1000000 [--fps 10]`, or one `<side>=<spec>` per side: `python -m rllab replay predator=runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@10 prey=scripted --env hunt --seed 1000000`
152	**Purpose:** Watch any saved agent, generation or baseline, one per side, play one seeded game frame by frame, with pause, step and speed controls.
153	**Covers:** REPLAY-01–REPLAY-05, CORE-04, CORE-06, CORE-07, CORE-10, AGENT-06, PG-12, SELF-07, QR-04
154	
155	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
156	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
157	| 1 | `replay_specs` | replay.specs | spec words → AgentSpecs | a lone word stays one spec; each `<side>=<spec>` word becomes one side's entry; a side named twice refused naming it | REPLAY-01 |
158	| 2 | `record_replay` | replay.record | AgentSpecs, env name, seed → Replay | `make_env` with the first run folder's own `env_options` (defaults for baselines only), `load_agent` per side, then `play_game` greedily with frames; a side with no spec raises `MissingSideError` before any window opens | REPLAY-01, REPLAY-04, REPLAY-05, CORE-10 |
159	| 3 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
160	| 4 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built | CORE-06 |
161	| 5 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01, SELF-07 |
162	| 6 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
163	| 7 | `RandomAgent` | baselines.uniform | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
164	| 8 | `ScriptedAgent` | baselines.scripted | name → Agent | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
165	| 9 | `ScriptedFish` | baselines.fish | name → Agent | PL-02 as built | REPLAY-01 |
166	| 10 | `ScriptedPredator` | baselines.predator | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
167	| 11 | `ScriptedPrey` | baselines.prey | name → Agent | PL-01 as built | REPLAY-01 |
168	| 12 | `DQNAgent` | agent.dqn | path, Side → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built (`DQNAgent.load`) | AGENT-06 |
169	| 13 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | path, Side → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built (`PGAgent.load`) | PG-12, SELF-07 |
170	| 14 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Lineup, seed → GameResult | PL-01 as built, with frames | CORE-04 |
171	| 15 | `show` | replay.window | Replay → Playback | pygame-ce window drawing each frame's cells, then its circles and segments, with step, each side's score and caption, at a fixed fps (AD-06) | REPLAY-02, REPLAY-03, QR-04 |
172	
173	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
174	```python
175	def run_replay(args: Namespace) -> Playback:
176	    specs = replay_specs(args.agents)
177	    replay = record_replay(specs, args.env, args.seed)
178	    return show(replay, fps=args.fps)
179	```

197	## PL-07: Build a cross-play table
198	**Entry:** `src/rllab/crossplay/pipeline.py::run_crossplay` · **Trigger:** `python -m rllab crossplay runs/hunt/selfplay-s1 runs/hunt/selfplay-s2 --out crossplay-selfplay.md [--title selfplay]`
199	**Purpose:** Play every saved generation of one side against every saved generation of the other on the same 20 held-out games, from the saved files only, and get the table, averaged over the runs given, as Markdown.
200	**Covers:** CROSS-01, CROSS-02, SELF-07, QR-04
201	
202	| # | Step | Owner | In → Out | Spine version | Covers |
203	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
204	| 1 | `gather_tables` | crossplay.tables | run paths → CrossTable (one per run) | per run, `load_table` when its `crossplay.json` exists, else `build_table`; a run with no generations refused, naming it | CROSS-02 |
205	| 2 | `load_table` | crossplay.store | path → CrossTable | read `crossplay.json` | CROSS-02 |
206	| 3 | `build_table` | crossplay.build | run folder → CrossTable | `load_record`, `make_env` with its `env_options`, each saved generation by `load_agent("<run>@<n>", env, side)`, every predator generation against every prey generation with `play_game(explore=False)` on the first 20 `EVAL_SEEDS`; both sides' per-game scores written to `run/crossplay.json`; never trains (AD-23) | CROSS-01, CROSS-02 |
207	| 4 | `load_record` | runs.store | path → RunRecord | PL-02 as built | CROSS-01 |
208	| 5 | `make_env` | registry.envs | env name, options → Env | PL-01 as built | CROSS-01 |
209	| 6 | `load_agent` | registry.agents | agent spec, Env, side → Agent | PL-01 as built | SELF-07 |
210	| 7 | `agent_path` | runs.paths | run folder, side, generation → AgentFile | PL-01 as built | SELF-07 |
211	| 8 | `PGAgent` | pg.agent | path, Side → LearningAgent | PL-01 as built (`PGAgent.load`) | SELF-07 |
212	| 9 | `play_game` | evaluate.play | Env, Lineup, seed → GameResult | PL-01 as built | CROSS-01 |
213	| 10 | `write_table` | crossplay.markdown | CrossTable, out, title → TablePage | one Markdown table: a row per predator generation, a column per prey generation, each cell `predator mean / prey mean` over every table's games; incomplete runs listed under it and in the result | CROSS-02, QR-04 |
214	
215	### Entry sketch (pre-build — removed by spine task)
216	```python
217	def run_crossplay(args: Namespace) -> TablePage:
218	    tables = gather_tables(args.runs)
219	    return write_table(tables, args.out, title=args.title)
220	```

# rllab — Contract

**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)

## core

### core.Choices: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Choices: n: int`
- **Invariants:** `n` ≥ 1; a move is an `int` in `range(n)` (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Choices/ · **Cases:** Snake's 3 moves; the compass tank's 8 moves
- **Used by:** snake, corridor, tank, agent, baselines, evaluate, registry

### core.Controls: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Controls: names: tuple[str, ...]; low: tuple[float, ...]; high: tuple[float, ...]`
- **Invariants:** the three tuples have the same length ≥ 1; `low[i] < high[i]`; a move is a float64 array of length `len(names)` with `low ≤ move ≤ high` element-wise (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Controls/ · **Cases:** the tank's and the prey's `("turn", "speed")`, −15 to 15 degrees and 0 to 0.3; the predator's −10 to 10 degrees and 0 to 0.35; the line's `("push",)`, −1 to 1
- **Used by:** tank, hunt, line, chase, pg, baselines, evaluate, registry

### core.Moves: type
`Moves: TypeAlias = Choices | Controls`
- **Invariants:** every side reports exactly one (CORE-03)
- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Moves/ · **Cases:** `Choices(3)`; the tank's `Controls`
- **Used by:** core.Side, core.InvalidMoveError, registry

### core.Move: type
`Move: TypeAlias = int | np.ndarray`
- **Invariants:** an `int` for `Choices`; a float64 array shaped `(len(names),)` for `Controls`
- **Boundary:** every environment and agent · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Move/ · **Cases:** Snake move 2; tank move `[15.0, 0.3]`; predator move `[-10.0, 0.35]`
- **Used by:** core.Env, core.Agent, core.Transition, evaluate, train

### core.Side: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Side: name: str; obs_size: int; moves: Moves; ids: tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `name` is a lowercase word, unique among the environment's sides; `ids` is non-empty, ascending and disjoint from every other side's; every observation of an id in `ids` has length `obs_size`, and its moves follow `moves` (CORE-03, AD-19)
- **Boundary:** every environment, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Side/ · **Cases:** Snake's `Side("snake", 11, Choices(3), (0,))`; the 5-fish tank's `fish` side, ids 0–4; the hunt's `predator` side (17, the predator's controls, id 0) and `prey` side (34, the tank's controls, ids 1–5)
- **Used by:** core.Env, agent, pg, evaluate, train, registry, crossplay

### core.Scores: type
`Scores: TypeAlias = dict[str, float]`
- **Invariants:** one entry per side name: Snake food eaten; the tank's fish mean steps survived; the hunt's predator prey caught and prey mean steps survived (HUNT-10, HUNT-11); the line's points that reached the end; the corridor 1 if it reached the end; the chase's chaser 1 if it touched, runner steps untouched
- **Boundary:** every environment, evaluate, runs, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Scores/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": 4.0}`; `{"predator": 3.0, "prey": 550.0}`
- **Used by:** core.Step, core.Frame, evaluate, runs, replay

### core.Env: type
```python
class Env(Protocol):
    name: str; sides: tuple[Side, ...]
    def reset(self, seed: int) -> dict[int, np.ndarray]: ...
    def step(self, moves: Mapping[int, Move]) -> Step: ...
    def render(self) -> Frame: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `reset` returns one float32 observation per id of every side, of its side's `obs_size`; `step` takes one move per living id (CORE-02); the same seed and moves give identical steps and frames (CORE-01, CORE-02); Snake, the corridor, the tank and the line have one side, the hunt and the chase two (AD-19)
- **Errors:** a move outside its side's `moves` raises `InvalidMoveError`; a missing move for a living id, or a move for one that isn't, raises `WrongAgentsError`; either way the game doesn't change; `step` after the game ends raises `GameOverError`
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Env/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 6-step corridor game; a replay double of a recorded Snake game on seed 7 ending in a wall hit; a replay double of a recorded 2-point line game where one point is removed first; a replay double of a recorded 2-prey hunt game on seed 7 where the predator catches one prey
- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, baselines, agent, pg, crossplay

### core.Step: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: dict[int, np.ndarray]; rewards: dict[int, float]; alive: frozenset[int]; done: bool; scores: Scores`
- **Invariants:** `obs` and `rewards` hold one entry for each id that was sent a move; `alive` ⊆ those ids, the ids asked for moves next (CORE-08); `done` exactly when `alive` is empty, including at a time limit, and the end of a game empties it (the hunt's predator is alive until then); `scores` is every side's score so far
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a Snake step that eats food (+10); a game-ending Snake step (−10, done); a corridor step that reaches the right end (+1, done); a tank step where 1 of 3 fish is removed; a line step where the first point reaches 5; a hunt step where the predator catches 2 prey (+2) and a prey eats a pellet
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.Circle: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Circle: x: float; y: float; r: float; color: str; fill: bool`
- **Invariants:** in the frame's cell units, origin at the top-left, y down; `color` is `#rrggbb`
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Circle/ · **Cases:** the tank wall (radius 10 at (10, 10), not filled); the ball; a pellet
- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay

### core.Segment: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Segment: x1: float; y1: float; x2: float; y2: float; color: str`
- **Invariants:** as `Circle`'s units and colour
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Segment/ · **Cases:** a fish's heading line; a prey's energy bar
- **Used by:** core.Frame, replay

### core.Frame: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: cells: np.ndarray; colors: dict[int, str]; step: int; scores: Scores; shapes: tuple[Circle | Segment, ...] = (); caption: str = ""`
- **Invariants:** `cells` is H×W uint8 cell codes and sets the drawing area; every code has a `#rrggbb` colour in `colors`; `shapes` are drawn over the cells in order; `caption` is extra text shown with the step and scores (CORE-04, AD-06); Snake uses codes 0 empty, 1 body, 2 head, 3 food and no shapes; the tank uses a blank 20 × 20 grid, its wall, ball and each living fish with a heading segment, and the caption `fish left: N`; the hunt the same grid and wall, its 10 pellets, the predator with a heading segment and each living prey with a heading segment and an energy bar of length 0.6 × energy, and the caption `prey left: N`
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** the first frame of Snake seed 7; a 1 × 5 corridor frame; the first frame of tank seed 2 with 5 fish; the first frame of hunt seed 2 with 5 prey
- **Used by:** replay, evaluate

### core.Transition: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: np.ndarray; action: Move; reward: float; next_obs: np.ndarray; done: bool; agent: int = 0`
- **Invariants:** one id's step: `obs` and `next_obs` have the same length; `done` is true on the step that id is removed or the game ends; `agent` is the id it acted as
- **Boundary:** train, agent, pg · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Transition/ · **Cases:** a non-final Snake step with reward 0; a final Snake step with reward −10; a tank fish's removal step (reward 0, done, agent 2); a predator's catch step (reward +1, agent 0)
- **Used by:** agent, pg, train

### core.Agent: type
```python
class Agent(Protocol):
    name: str; kind: Literal["choices", "controls", "any"]
    def reset(self, seed: int) -> None: ...
    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, env: Env, *, explore: bool) -> Move: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `kind` says which `Moves` it plays; `reset` is called once at the start of every game with that game's seed; `act` is called once per living id of the side it plays, in id order, on that id's observation, so one object plays every fish of a side with one policy (PG-04); with `explore=False`, the same seed and observations give the same moves (AGENT-03, PG-02, REPLAY-04)
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, baselines, evaluate, train, replay, selfplay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Agent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded random-agent game on corridor seed 3; a replay double of a recorded random-agent tank game on seed 2 with 2 fish
- **Used by:** evaluate, train, replay, registry, selfplay

### core.Lineup: type
`Lineup: TypeAlias = Mapping[str, Agent]`
- **Invariants:** one agent per side name of the environment it plays; one agent may be named for more than one side (AD-19)
- **Boundary:** evaluate, train, registry, selfplay, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Lineup/ · **Cases:** `{"snake": the scripted agent}`; `{"predator": a rung-3 agent, "prey": the scripted prey}`
- **Used by:** core.Match, core.check_agent, evaluate, train, registry

### core.LearningAgent: type
```python
class LearningAgent(Agent, Protocol):
    def begin_game(self, game: int, n_games: int) -> None: ...
    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> float | None: ...
    def end_game(self) -> float | None: ...
    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...
```
- **Invariants:** called only for games in which its side learns: `begin_game` sets up training game `game` of `n_games`, `learn` is called for each of its side's ids' steps and `end_game` once after the game; each returns the loss of the update it made, or `None` when it made none (AD-12, AD-20); in a game where its side is frozen it only acts
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.LearningAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game corridor DQN run, with losses from `learn`; a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of rung 1, with losses from `end_game`
- **Used by:** train, registry, selfplay

### core.Match: type
`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Match: agents: Lineup; learning: frozenset[str]; label: str = ""`
- **Invariants:** `agents` names one agent per side; each side in `learning` plays the run's own learner for that side, and only those learners are told about the game; every other side plays frozen (CORE-10, AD-20); `label` names the kind of game (`both`, `past-prey`, `past-predator`, `block-3`)
- **Boundary:** train, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Match/ · **Cases:** a one-sided Snake game learning `{"snake"}`; the current predator against prey generation 3, learning `{"predator"}`; a taking-turns block-1 game learning `{"prey"}`
- **Used by:** core.Schedule, train, selfplay

### core.Schedule: type
```python
class Schedule(Protocol):
    def match(self, game: int) -> Match: ...
    def add_generation(self, side: str, number: int, path: Path) -> None: ...
```
- **Invariants:** `match` is called once before each training game, in game order; `add_generation` after each generation is saved, with its file; the same run seed gives the same matches (QR-05, AD-22)
- **Boundary:** train, selfplay, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Schedule/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 20-game self-play schedule with generations added after games 10 and 20; a replay double of a recorded 20-game taking-turns schedule
- **Used by:** train, registry

### core.InvalidMoveError: type
`class InvalidMoveError(ValueError): agent: int; side: str; move: Move; allowed: Moves; step: int | None`
- **Invariants:** the message names the id, its side, the move and the allowed choices or each control with its limits, and, when raised by `evaluate.play_game`, the step number
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.InvalidMoveError/ · **Cases:** Snake given move 3; the tank given a turn of 20; the predator given a turn of 12
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.WrongAgentsError: type
`class WrongAgentsError(ValueError): missing: tuple[int, ...]; extra: tuple[int, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `missing` names the living ids with no move, `extra` the ids sent a move that aren't living; the message names each fish (TANK and HUNT edge cases)
- **Boundary:** tank, hunt, line, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WrongAgentsError/ · **Cases:** a move for removed fish 1; living fish 3 with no move; no move for the predator
- **Used by:** evaluate, train

### core.GameOverError: type
`class GameOverError(ValueError): ...`
- **Invariants:** the message says the game is over
- **Boundary:** snake, corridor, tank, hunt, line, chase, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.GameOverError/ · **Cases:** a step after a Snake wall hit; a step after the tank's last fish is removed; a step after the hunt's last prey is removed
- **Used by:** evaluate

### core.EnvCheckError: type
`class EnvCheckError(TypeError): missing: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `missing` lists every absent behaviour by attribute name, and `sides` when the sides are empty or two share a name or an id
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EnvCheckError/ · **Cases:** an object missing `render` and `step`; two sides sharing id 0
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay

### core.MoveKindError: type
`class MoveKindError(TypeError): agent: str; agent_kind: str; side: str; env: str; env_kind: str`
- **Invariants:** the message names the agent and its kind, and the environment, the side and its kind (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MoveKindError/ · **Cases:** the DQN given the continuous tank; a rung given Snake
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

### core.MissingSideError: type
`class MissingSideError(ValueError): env: str; sides: tuple[str, ...]`
- **Invariants:** `sides` names every side with no agent; the message names them and the environment (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MissingSideError/ · **Cases:** the hunt given only a predator; a lone agent given to the chase
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry

### core.NonFiniteLossError: type
`class NonFiniteLossError(FloatingPointError): update: int`
- **Invariants:** `update` is the agent's update count; `train.train_run` adds the game and step to the failed record's `error`
- **Boundary:** agent, pg, train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.NonFiniteLossError/ · **Cases:** a NaN loss on DQN update 12; an infinite loss on a rung's update 3
- **Used by:** agent, pg, train

### core.check_env: Start-up environment check
`def check_env(env: object) -> Env`
- **Invariants:** returns `env` unchanged when it has `name`, `sides`, `reset`, `step` and `render`, and its sides are non-empty with distinct names and disjoint ids
- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError` naming every missing behaviour, or `sides` (CORE edge case)
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_env/ · **Cases:** a corridor env that passes; an object missing `render`; a two-sided env whose sides share id 0
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay; PL-01

### core.check_agent: Start-up agents and environment match
`def check_agent(agents: Agent | Lineup, env: Env) -> Lineup`
- **Invariants:** a lone `Agent` on a one-sided environment becomes `{side.name: agent}`; returns a lineup with one agent per side, each of kind `"any"` or matching its side's moves (`Choices` → `"choices"`, `Controls` → `"controls"`)
- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` naming each side with no agent, a lone agent on a two-sided environment included (CORE edge case); `MoveKindError` naming both kinds; `ValueError` naming a lineup key that isn't a side
- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.check_agent/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the tank; the DQN on the continuous tank; the hunt with only a predator; both sides of the hunt at rung 3
- **Used by:** train, evaluate, registry, crossplay; PL-01

120	### chase.ChaseEnv: Two-sided test environment
121	`class ChaseEnv(Env): def __init__(self, length: float = 5.0, max_steps: int = 20) -> None; name = "chase"`
122	- **Invariants:** sides `Side("chaser", 2, Controls(("push",), (-1.0,), (1.0,)), (0,))` and `Side("runner", 2, the same controls, (1,))`; each observes its own position / `length`, then the other's; the chaser starts at 0, the runner at `length`; each step both move by their push, held within `[0, length]`; when they are within 0.5 after a step the chaser gets +1, the runner 0 and the game ends; otherwise the runner gets +1 and the chaser 0; `max_steps` also ends it; scores are the chaser's touches (0 or 1) and the runner's steps untouched (CORE-10)
123	- **Errors:** as `core.Env`
124	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/chase.ChaseEnv/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a game where the chaser touches the runner on step 4; a replay double of a game where the runner lasts all 20 steps

127	### pg.PGConfig: type
128	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PGConfig: rung: Literal[1, 2, 3] = 3; value: bool = True; cap: bool = True; rescale: bool = True; hidden: int = 64; lr: float = 3e-4; gamma: float = 0.99; lam: float = 0.95; passes: int = 10; batch: int = 64; clip: float = 0.2; log_std_init: float = -0.5`
129	- **Invariants:** rung 1 is plain policy gradient, 2 actor-critic, 3 the limited-update agent (AD-13); `value`, `cap` and `rescale` are the tank's switches (ABL-06–ABL-08) and only rung 3 has them
130	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the switch and the rung if a switch is off on rung 1 or 2 (ABL edge case)
131	- **Boundary:** runs, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGConfig/ · **Cases:** each rung's default; rung 3 with each switch off alone; rung 1 with `cap` off (refused)
132	- **Used by:** runs, study, registry
133	
134	### pg.Spread: type
135	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Spread: centre: np.ndarray; width: np.ndarray`
136	- **Invariants:** one entry per control; `width` > 0 is the standard deviation of a normal draw around `centre` (PG-01)
137	- **Boundary:** pg tests · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.Spread/ · **Cases:** an untrained rung's spread on a tank observation
138	- **Used by:** pg tests
139	
140	### pg.PGAgent: Hand-written policy-gradient ladder
141	```python
142	class PGAgent(LearningAgent):
143	    def __init__(self, obs_size: int, controls: Controls, config: PGConfig, *, seed: int) -> None: ...
144	    def policy(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> Spread: ...
145	    def value(self, obs: np.ndarray) -> float: ...
146	    @classmethod
147	    def load(cls, path: Path, side: Side, *, seed: int | None = None) -> "PGAgent": ...
148	```
149	- **Invariants:** `kind = "controls"`; one agent per side, learning only from the transitions it is handed, its own side's (PG-04, SELF-01); training moves are drawn from `policy(obs)`, evaluation moves are its centre, and both are clipped to the limits before they are sent (PG-01–PG-03); `learn` stores the step and returns `None`; `end_game` learns once from every stored step of every fish, then clears them (PG-04, PG-05), as AD-13 sets per rung and switch (PG-06–PG-09, ABL-06–ABL-08); a loaded agent picks the same moves (PG-12, SELF-07), and its training draws come from a generator built from `seed`, or from the saved run seed when `seed` is `None` (AD-22)
150	- **Errors:** `value` raises `ValueError` on a rung with no value estimate; `load` raises `ValueError` naming both observation sizes and controls if `side` doesn't match the file, another side's generation included (SELF edge case); `end_game` raises `core.NonFiniteLossError`
151	- **Boundary:** train, registry, selfplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/pg.PGAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 3-game line run of each rung; a saved rung 3 loaded back; a saved predator generation loaded back; a prey generation loaded for the predator side (refused)
152	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04, PL-05, PL-07
153	
154	## baselines
155	
156	### baselines.RandomAgent: Uniform random baseline
157	`class RandomAgent(Agent): name = "random"; kind = "any"`
158	- **Invariants:** picks uniformly from `range(n)` for `Choices`, and each control uniformly within its limits for `Controls` (BASE-01), reading the limits of the side of the id it is asked for, with a generator reseeded by `reset(seed)`, so a replay on the same seed is identical (REPLAY-04)
159	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of 10 moves on Snake seed 42; a replay double of 10 moves in the tank on seed 2; a replay double of 10 predator moves on hunt seed 2
160	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-02, PL-05
161	
162	### baselines.ScriptedAgent: Greedy, danger-avoiding Snake baseline
163	`class ScriptedAgent(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "choices"`
164	- **Invariants:** reads `env.state`; steps toward the food along the axis with the larger gap, and if that move is immediately fatal takes a non-fatal move, preferring straight; if every move is fatal it goes straight (BASE-02); deterministic
165	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if `env` isn't a `SnakeEnv`
166	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedAgent/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a Snake seed 42 game; food ahead with a wall ahead
167	- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05
168	
169	### baselines.ScriptedFish: Ball-fleeing tank baseline
170	`class ScriptedFish(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
171	- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `tank.TankEnv`'s layout; always speed 0.3; with the wall within 1 unit ahead (forward wall reading ≤ 0.25), turns 15° toward the side whose rays read more room, left winning a tie; else away from the ray seeing the ball closest: rays 1–3 → −15°, rays 5–7 → +15°, ray 0 → toward the side with more room, ray 4 → straight; on equal ball readings the further-left ray counts; no ball in reach → straight (BASE-04); deterministic
172	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment if it isn't the continuous tank
173	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedFish/ · **Cases:** the ball on the front-left ray only; no ball and the wall 0.5 ahead with more room on the left; the ball and the wall ahead at once
174	- **Used by:** registry; PL-02, PL-05
175	
176	### baselines.ScriptedPredator: Prey-chasing hunt baseline
177	`class ScriptedPredator(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
178	- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `hunt.HuntEnv`'s predator layout; always speed 0.35; with a prey in reach, turns toward the ray seeing it closest: rays 1–3 → +10°, rays 5–7 → −10°, ray 0 → 0°, ray 4 → +10°; else with the wall within 1 unit ahead (forward wall reading ≤ 0.25), turns 10° toward the side whose rays read more room, left winning a tie; else straight (BASE-05); on equal prey readings the further-left ray counts; deterministic
179	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment and side if it isn't the hunt's predator
180	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPredator/ · **Cases:** a prey on the front-left ray only; no prey and the wall 0.5 ahead with more room on the right; equal prey readings on rays 1 and 7
181	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-02, PL-05
182	
183	### baselines.ScriptedPrey: Wall-, predator- and pellet-led hunt baseline
184	`class ScriptedPrey(Agent): name = "scripted"; kind = "controls"`
185	- **Invariants:** reads only the observation, in `hunt.HuntEnv`'s prey layout; always speed 0.3; first, with the wall within 1 unit ahead, turns 15° toward the side with more room, left winning a tie; else with the predator in reach, away from the ray seeing it closest, as `ScriptedFish` flees the ball; else with a pellet in reach, toward the ray seeing it closest: rays 1–3 → +15°, rays 5–7 → −15°, ray 0 → 0°, ray 4 → +15°; else straight (BASE-06); on equal readings the further-left ray counts; deterministic
186	- **Errors:** `TypeError` naming the environment and side if it isn't the hunt's prey
187	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.ScriptedPrey/ · **Cases:** the predator on the front-left ray only; no predator and a pellet on the right ray only; the predator and the wall ahead at once
188	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-02, PL-05
189	
190	## evaluate
191	
192	### evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS: Held-out seeds
193	`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_100))`
194	- **Invariants:** 100 seeds; disjoint from every training seed (EVAL-01); regular evaluation points use the first `RunConfig.eval_games` of them (EVAL-02), and the cross-play table the first 20 (CROSS-01)
195	- **Boundary:** train, study, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100-seed tuple
196	- **Used by:** train, study, replay, crossplay
197	
198	### evaluate.Seeds: type
199	`Seeds: TypeAlias = tuple[int, ...]`
200	- **Invariants:** game seeds in the order the games are played; every seed is in `[0, 1_000_000)`, so none is a held-out seed (EVAL-01)
201	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Seeds/ · **Cases:** the 1,000 training seeds of run seed 1; the 200 training seeds of run seed 2
202	- **Used by:** train
203	
204	### evaluate.training_seeds: Training game seeds
205	`def training_seeds(run_seed: int, n_games: int) -> Seeds`
206	- **Invariants:** `n_games` seeds in `[0, 1_000_000)`, the same for the same `run_seed`
207	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `n_games` < 1
208	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.training_seeds/ · **Cases:** seed 1 with 1,000 games
209	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
210	
211	### evaluate.GameResult: type
212	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameResult: seed: int; scores: Scores; length: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]`
213	- **Invariants:** `scores` holds every side's final score; `length` is the number of environment steps; `frames` is empty unless requested, otherwise `length + 1` frames from the first to the last
214	- **Boundary:** train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.GameResult/ · **Cases:** a corridor game with frames; a Snake game without frames; a tank game with frames; a hunt game where the predator catches 3 and 2 prey starve
215	- **Used by:** train, replay, crossplay
216	
217	### evaluate.EvalResult: type
218	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalResult: seeds: tuple[int, ...]; scores: tuple[float, ...]; mean: float; std: float`
219	- **Invariants:** one side's scores, one per seed, in seed order; `std` is the population standard deviation
220	- **Boundary:** runs, train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.EvalResult/ · **Cases:** the random agent on 100 held-out Snake seeds; a 10-seed corridor evaluation; the scripted fish on the first 20 held-out tank seeds; the scripted predator's catches against the scripted prey on 100 held-out hunt seeds
221	- **Used by:** runs, train, study
222	
223	### evaluate.play_game: Play one game
224	`def play_game(env: Env, agents: Agent | Lineup, seed: int, *, explore: bool = False, frames: bool = False) -> GameResult`
225	- **Invariants:** `check_agent` first; calls `env.reset(seed)` and each distinct agent's `reset(seed)` once, then each step asks every living id, in id order, its side's agent for a move and stops asking a removed one (CORE-08), until done; the same inputs give the same result
226	- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` and `MoveKindError` before the game; `InvalidMoveError` naming the id, the side, the move and the step number if a move is outside its side's moves (CORE edge case)
227	- **Boundary:** train, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.play_game/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42 with frames; an agent returning move 5; the random agent on line seed 3; the scripted predator against the scripted prey on hunt seed 2
228	- **Used by:** train, replay, crossplay, evaluate.evaluate; PL-01, PL-02, PL-05, PL-07
229	
230	### evaluate.evaluate: Greedy score on a seed set
231	`def evaluate(env: Env, agents: Agent | Lineup, seeds: Sequence[int] = EVAL_SEEDS, *, side: str | None = None) -> EvalResult`
232	- **Invariants:** one `play_game(..., explore=False)` per seed, scoring `side`, or the only side when `None`; changes no agent state (EVAL-02, BASE-03)
233	- **Errors:** `MissingSideError` and `MoveKindError` before any game; `ValueError` if `side` is `None` on a two-sided environment or isn't one of its sides
234	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.evaluate/ · **Cases:** the random agent on the 100 held-out Snake seeds; a rung-3 predator against the scripted prey on the first 20 held-out hunt seeds, scoring `predator`
235	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02
236	
237	## selfplay
238	
239	### selfplay.SelfPlayConfig: type
240	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class SelfPlayConfig: rung: Literal["turns", "simultaneous", "selfplay"]; first: str; blocks: int = 10; both: float = 0.5`
241	- **Invariants:** `first` is the side that learns first when taking turns (the hunt: `prey`, SELF-03); `blocks` applies to `turns`; `both` is the share of self-play games played current against current, the rest split evenly between the two kinds against a past generation (SELF-05); `0 < both < 1`
242	- **Boundary:** runs, study, registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.SelfPlayConfig/ · **Cases:** the hunt's `turns`, `simultaneous` and `selfplay` configs
243	- **Used by:** runs, study, registry
244	
245	### selfplay.make_schedule: The match for each training game
246	`def make_schedule(config: SelfPlayConfig, learners: Mapping[str, LearningAgent], n_games: int, *, seed: int, load: Callable[[Path, str, int], Agent]) -> Schedule`
247	- **Invariants:** `turns`: game g is in block `g · blocks // n_games`, block 0 learning `first` and the sides alternating, the other side playing its current learner frozen (SELF-03); `simultaneous`: every game both current, both learning (SELF-04); `selfplay`: current against current while the opponent has no generation, else a kind drawn with shares `both`, `(1 − both) / 2`, `(1 − both) / 2`, and the past generation uniformly from those added so far, from a generator built from `seed`; a past generation is `load(path, side, seed)`ed once, cached, plays frozen and never learns (SELF-05, AD-22)
248	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `learners` doesn't have exactly 2 sides, `first` isn't one of them, or `blocks` doesn't divide `n_games`
249	- **Boundary:** registry · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/selfplay.make_schedule/ · **Cases:** a 20-game self-play schedule on seed 1 with generations added after games 2 and 4; a 20-game taking-turns schedule; 10,000 self-play matches after 4 prey generations, for the counts SELF-05 checks
250	- **Used by:** registry; PL-01, PL-04
251	
252	## runs
253	
254	### runs.AgentSpec: type
255	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgentSpec: kind: Literal["dqn", "pg", "random", "scripted"]; config: AgentConfig | PGConfig | None = None`
256	- **Invariants:** `config` is an `AgentConfig` exactly when `kind == "dqn"`, a `PGConfig` exactly when `"pg"`, else `None`; `dqn` and `pg` are learners (LOG-01)
257	- **Boundary:** train, study, registry, curves · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.AgentSpec/ · **Cases:** the full DQN; rung 3 with the cap off; the scripted baseline
258	- **Used by:** runs.RunConfig, study.Variant, registry
259	
260	### runs.RunConfig: type
261	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; env: str; agents: dict[str, AgentSpec]; seed: int; n_games: int = 1_000; eval_every: int = 50; eval_games: int = 100; generations: int = 0; selfplay: SelfPlayConfig | None = None; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...] = ()`
262	- **Invariants:** `agents` has one entry per side of `env`; the learning sides are those with a learner; a baseline run has `n_games == 0` and no learner; `eval_games` ≤ 100; `generations` is 0 or divides `n_games`, and is 10 for every hunt training run (SELF-02); `selfplay` is set exactly when two sides learn (AD-20); `env_options` are `make_env`'s keyword arguments (tank: `fish`, `compass`; hunt: `prey`); `name` is a valid folder name (LOG-01)
263	- **Boundary:** train, study, registry, curves · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** the full Snake agent, seed 1; the memory-off agent, seed 3; the scripted Snake baseline; rung 3 with the cap off in a 5-fish tank, `eval_every` 100 and `eval_games` 20; a hunt self-play run on seed 2, both sides at rung 3 with 10 generations; the hunt's random-predator baseline (a random predator against the scripted prey)
264	- **Used by:** train, study, registry, curves
265	
266	### runs.RunRecord: type
267	```python
268	@dataclass(frozen=True)
269	class RunRecord:
270	    config: RunConfig; status: Literal["running", "finished", "failed"]; error: str | None; elapsed_s: float
271	    games: tuple[GameStat, ...]; evals: tuple[EvalPoint, ...]; losses: tuple[LossPoint, ...]
272	    final: dict[str, EvalResult] | None; generations: dict[str, tuple[int, ...]]
273	```
274	- **Invariants:** `games[i]` is training game i (LOG-02); an evaluation and a loss point every `eval_every` games (LOG-03); `final` holds one result per scored side over all `EVAL_SEEDS` (each learning side against the other sides' evaluation opponents; for a baseline, every side of its lineup) and is set exactly when `status == "finished"` (EVAL-02, AD-15); `generations` lists each learning side's saved generation numbers in order, empty when `config.generations` is 0 (SELF-02); `error` names the game and step when `status == "failed"`
275	- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry, replay, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished 200-game corridor run; a Snake run failed at game 430 (evaluation points to game 400); a baseline record; a record with no evaluation points; a finished tank run with 10 points over 20 seeds and a final over 100; a finished hunt self-play run with 10 points and 10 generations per side; a hunt run failed after 6 generations
276	- **Used by:** curves, study, registry, crossplay
277	
278	### runs.GameStat: type
279	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class GameStat: scores: Scores; length: int`
280	- **Invariants:** every side's score; `length` ≥ 1
281	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.GameStat/ · **Cases:** a Snake game scoring 4 in 212 steps; a tank game scoring 550 in 1,000 steps; a hunt game, predator 3 and prey 412, in 730 steps
282	- **Used by:** curves, study
283	
284	### runs.EvalPoint: type
285	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: game: int; results: dict[str, EvalResult]`
286	- **Invariants:** `game` is the number of training games played before this evaluation, a multiple of `eval_every`; one result per learning side
287	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** the Snake evaluation after game 50; a hunt evaluation after game 100 with both sides
288	- **Used by:** curves, study
289	
290	### runs.LossPoint: type
291	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class LossPoint: game: int; mean_loss: dict[str, float]`
292	- **Invariants:** per learning side, the mean of the losses its learner's `learn` and `end_game` returned over the last `eval_every` games; NaN for a side that never learned in that window
293	- **Boundary:** curves, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.LossPoint/ · **Cases:** the Snake loss after game 100; a taking-turns window where the predator didn't learn
294	- **Used by:** curves, study
295	
296	### runs.RunExistsError: type
297	`class RunExistsError(FileExistsError): name: str`
298	- **Invariants:** the message names the existing run and its environment
299	- **Boundary:** train, study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunExistsError/ · **Cases:** creating `snake/full-s1` twice
300	- **Used by:** train, study, cli
301	
302	### runs.RunWriter: type
303	```python
304	class RunWriter(Protocol):
305	    path: Path
306	    def add_game(self, scores: Scores, length: int) -> None: ...
307	    def add_eval(self, game: int, results: Mapping[str, EvalResult]) -> None: ...  # also flushes
308	    def add_loss(self, game: int, mean_loss: Mapping[str, float]) -> None: ...
309	    def save_agent(self, side: str, agent: LearningAgent) -> Path: ...
310	    def save_generation(self, side: str, number: int, agent: LearningAgent) -> Path: ...  # also flushes
311	    def finish(self, status: Literal["finished", "failed"], error: str | None = None, final: Mapping[str, EvalResult] | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
312	```
313	- **Invariants:** `record.json` is atomically rewritten after every `add_eval` and `save_generation` and in `finish` (LOG-05); `save_agent` and `save_generation` write the files `agent_path` names (LOG-04, SELF-02); `finish("finished")` requires `final`; a failed run keeps the generations saved so far (SELF edge case)
314	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 100-game run with 2 evaluation points and a final; a replay double of a recorded 20-game chase run with 2 generations per side
315	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01
316	
317	### runs.AgentFile: type
318	`AgentFile: TypeAlias = Path`
319	- **Invariants:** a saved side's or generation's `torch.save` file: `run/<side>.pt` or `run/generations/<side>-<NN>.pt` (LOG-04, SELF-02, AD-05); naming one doesn't create it
320	- **Boundary:** registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.AgentFile/ · **Cases:** `runs/snake/full-s1/snake.pt`; `runs/hunt/selfplay-s1/generations/prey-03.pt`
321	- **Used by:** registry, crossplay; PL-01, PL-05, PL-07
322	
323	### runs.agent_path: Where a saved agent lives
324	`def agent_path(run: Path, side: str, generation: int | None = None) -> AgentFile`
325	- **Invariants:** `run/<side>.pt` for the final agent, `run/generations/<side>-<NN>.pt` for generation NN, two digits (LOG-04, AD-05)
326	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `generation` is outside 1–99
327	- **Boundary:** registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.agent_path/ · **Cases:** `runs/snake/full-s1`, `snake`; `runs/hunt/selfplay-s1`, `prey`, generation 3
328	- **Used by:** registry, crossplay; PL-01, PL-05, PL-07
329	
330	### runs.create_run: Start a run record
331	`def create_run(config: RunConfig, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter`
332	- **Invariants:** creates `root/config.env/config.name/` and writes the config to the record at once (LOG-01, AD-15)
333	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the existing run if the folder exists; nothing is overwritten
334	- **Boundary:** train, study · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.create_run/ · **Cases:** a new corridor run; an existing run name
335	- **Used by:** train, study; PL-01, PL-02
336	
337	### runs.load_record: Read a run record
338	`def load_record(path: Path) -> RunRecord`
339	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run if `record.json` is missing
340	- **Boundary:** curves, study, registry, crossplay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runs.load_record/ · **Cases:** a finished corridor record; a record failed at game 430; a finished hunt record
341	- **Used by:** curves, study, registry, crossplay; PL-02, PL-03, PL-04, PL-05, PL-07
342	
343	## train
344	
345	### train.train_run: Train one run's learners
346	`def train_run(env: Env, learners: LearningAgent | Mapping[str, LearningAgent], config: RunConfig, *, opponents: Lineup = {}, schedule: Schedule | None = None, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunRecord`
347	- **Invariants:** checks `env` and the lineup first; plays `config.n_games` games on `training_seeds(config.seed, n_games)`, each game `schedule.match(game)`'s match, or every learner playing and learning when `schedule` is `None`; for learning sides `begin_game`, then each step `act(explore=True)` per living id in id order, `env.step`, `learn` per learning side's `Transition` in id order, and `end_game` after the game (AD-12, AD-20); every `eval_every` games records, per learning side, `evaluate` on the first `eval_games` held-out seeds against `opponents` for the other sides, and each side's mean loss; every `n_games / generations` games saves a generation of each learning side and passes it to `schedule.add_generation` (SELF-02); finishes with `final` over all `EVAL_SEEDS`, reusing the last point when it already is one, and saves each learner; same config → same record apart from `elapsed_s` (QR-05); works for any `Env` (CORE-05, CORE-07, CORE-10)
348	- **Errors:** `EnvCheckError`, `MoveKindError`, `MissingSideError` (a side with no learner, or, in a two-sided run, no evaluation opponent) and `RunExistsError` before any game; on any later failure, including `NonFiniteLossError` and `InvalidMoveError`, finishes the record as `failed`, naming the game and step, keeps its generations, then re-raises
349	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.train_run/ · **Cases:** a 200-game corridor run, seed 1; a run that hits a non-finite loss; a 100-game line run of rung 1; a 50-game chase run with both sides learning; a chase run whose schedule keeps the runner frozen
350	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
351	
352	## registry
353	
354	### registry.make_env: Environment by name
355	`def make_env(name: str, **options: int) -> Env`
356	- **Invariants:** `"snake"` → `SnakeEnv()`, `"corridor"` → `CorridorEnv()`, `"tank"` → `TankEnv(fish=options.get("fish", 5), compass=bool(options.get("compass", 0)))`, `"hunt"` → `HuntEnv(prey=options.get("prey", 5))`, `"line"` → `LineEnv()`, `"chase"` → `ChaseEnv()`; the result passes `check_env`
357	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments; `TypeError` naming an option the environment doesn't take
358	- **Boundary:** study, replay, crossplay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_env/ · **Cases:** `snake`; `corridor`; `tank` with 3 fish; `tank` with `compass=1`; `hunt` with 5 prey; `chase`; an unknown name
359	- **Used by:** study, replay, crossplay, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04, PL-05, PL-07
360	
361	### registry.EvalSchedule: type
362	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalSchedule: every: int; games: int`
363	- **Invariants:** `every` ≥ 1; 1 ≤ `games` ≤ 100; fills `RunConfig.eval_every` and `RunConfig.eval_games` (AD-15)
364	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.EvalSchedule/ · **Cases:** Snake's `(50, 100)`; the tank's `(100, 20)` for 1,000 games; the tank's `(1, 20)` for 5 games
365	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
366	
367	### registry.eval_schedule: Evaluation cadence per environment
368	`def eval_schedule(env_name: str, n_games: int) -> EvalSchedule`
369	- **Invariants:** `EvalSchedule(50, 100)` for `snake` and `corridor`; `EvalSchedule(max(1, n_games // 10), 20)` for `tank`, `hunt`, `line` and `chase` (EVAL-02, AD-15)
370	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the known environments
371	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.eval_schedule/ · **Cases:** snake with 1,000 games; tank with 1,000 games; tank with 5 games; hunt with 1,000 games
372	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
373	
374	### registry.make_agent: One side's learner for a run
375	`def make_agent(config: RunConfig, env: Env, side: str) -> LearningAgent`
376	- **Invariants:** from `config.agents[side]` and `env`'s side of that name: `"dqn"` → `DQNAgent(side.obs_size, side.moves.n, spec.config, seed=config.seed)`; `"pg"` → `PGAgent(side.obs_size, side.moves, spec.config, seed=config.seed)`, so the predator and the prey each get their own (SELF-01); the result passes `check_agent` for that side
377	- **Errors:** `MoveKindError` naming both kinds (the DQN on the continuous tank, a rung on Snake); `ValueError` for a baseline kind or a name that isn't a side
378	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_agent/ · **Cases:** the full DQN on Snake; rung 3 on the tank; the DQN on the compass tank; the DQN on the continuous tank (refused); rung 3 for the hunt's predator
379	- **Used by:** registry.make_run, study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
380	
381	### registry.RunSetup: type
382	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunSetup: env: Env; learners: dict[str, LearningAgent]; opponents: Lineup; schedule: Schedule | None`
383	- **Invariants:** `learners` has one learner per learning side of the config; `opponents` holds, for a two-sided environment, each side's scripted agent, or the random agent where the side has none (the chase), and is empty for a one-sided one; `schedule` is set exactly when `config.selfplay` is (AD-20)
384	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.RunSetup/ · **Cases:** the full DQN on Snake; a hunt self-play run with both learners, both scripted opponents and its schedule; a chase run
385	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
386	
387	### registry.make_run: Everything a training run needs
388	`def make_run(config: RunConfig) -> RunSetup`
389	- **Invariants:** `make_env(config.env, **dict(config.env_options))`, `make_agent` for each learning side, the opponents, and with `config.selfplay` `selfplay.make_schedule(config.selfplay, learners, config.n_games, seed=config.seed, load=…)`, the loader loading a generation with `load_agent`'s rules (AD-22)
390	- **Errors:** as `make_env` and `make_agent`
391	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.make_run/ · **Cases:** the full Snake agent; a hunt self-play run on seed 2; a hunt simultaneous run
392	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-01, PL-04
393	
394	### registry.load_agent: Agent by name, run folder or generation
395	`def load_agent(spec: str, env: Env, side: str | None = None) -> Agent`
396	- **Invariants:** for `side`, or the only side when `None`: `"random"` gives `RandomAgent`; `"scripted"` gives `ScriptedAgent` on Snake, `ScriptedFish` on the continuous tank, and `ScriptedPredator` or `ScriptedPrey` on the hunt's sides (AD-08); a run folder gives that side's final agent and `<run folder>@<n>` its generation n, loaded with `DQNAgent.load` or `PGAgent.load` as the record's `AgentSpec` says (SELF-07, REPLAY-01)
397	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming the run or generation if it doesn't exist or has no saved agent; `ValueError` naming the environment and side if `"scripted"` has none there, or if `side` is `None` on a two-sided environment
398	- **Boundary:** replay, study, crossplay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/registry.load_agent/ · **Cases:** `random`; `scripted` on snake; `scripted` on the tank; `scripted` for the hunt's prey; a saved DQN run folder; a saved rung 3 run folder; `runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@10` for the predator; a missing run
399	- **Used by:** replay, study, crossplay, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-04, PL-05, PL-07

403	### curves.CurvePlot: type
404	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CurvePlot: path: Path; lines: tuple[str, ...]; skipped: tuple[str, ...]`
405	- **Invariants:** `lines` are the drawn labels, baselines included; `skipped` names every record with no evaluation points
406	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.CurvePlot/ · **Cases:** the full agent with 3 ablations and 2 baselines; one record skipped; the 3 rungs with 2 baselines; the predator's 3 self-play rungs with 2 baselines
407	- **Used by:** study, cli

409	### curves.plot_curves: Learning-curve chart
410	`def plot_curves(records: Sequence[RunRecord], out: Path, *, baselines: Sequence[RunRecord] = (), side: str | None = None) -> CurvePlot`
411	- **Invariants:** draws `side`'s evaluation results, or the only side's when `None`; one line per config minus seed: the mean evaluation score at each evaluation point, with a min–max band across seeds (CURVE-01, CURVE-02); each baseline record whose other sides all play `scripted` as a labelled flat line of `side`'s final mean (CURVE-03); labels name the agent and each switch that is off: `full` or the DQN's switches off (CURVE-04), a rung's name (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) plus any switch off (CURVE-06), a self-play rung's name (`turns`, `simultaneous`, `selfplay`, CURVE-07), `compass` added for the compass tank; reads only records, in ≤ 10 s (CURVE-05)
412	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the environments if the records come from more than one, or naming the sides if `side` is `None` for a two-sided environment or isn't one of its sides
413	- **Boundary:** study, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/curves.plot_curves/ · **Cases:** the full agent, 3 ablations and 2 baselines; 5 seeds of each rung and 2 tank baselines; 5 seeds of each self-play rung for the prey with the 3 hunt baseline records; records from two environments
414	- **Used by:** study, cli; PL-03, PL-04

416	## replay
417	
418	### replay.Replay: type
419	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Replay: agents: dict[str, str]; env: str; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; scores: Scores`
420	- **Invariants:** `agents` is the spec each side was loaded from; `frames` runs from the first frame to game over; `scores == frames[-1].scores`
421	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; the random agent on corridor seed 3; the scripted fish on tank seed 2; predator generation 10 against prey generation 3 on hunt seed 42
422	- **Used by:** cli
423	
424	### replay.AgentSpecs: type
425	`AgentSpecs: TypeAlias = str | Mapping[str, str]`
426	- **Invariants:** a lone agent spec on a one-sided environment, or one spec per side name; a spec is `random`, `scripted`, a run folder, or `<run>@<n>` for its generation n (REPLAY-01, SELF-07)
427	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.AgentSpecs/ · **Cases:** `scripted` alone; `{"predator": "runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@10", "prey": "runs/hunt/selfplay-s1@3"}`
428	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
429	
430	### replay.record_replay: Re-play a game
431	`def record_replay(agents: AgentSpecs, env_name: str, seed: int, **options: int) -> Replay`
432	- **Invariants:** a lone spec on a one-sided environment, or one per side (REPLAY-01); `make_env(env_name, **options)`, taking the first run folder's own `env_options` when none are given, then `load_agent` per side and `play_game(..., explore=False, frames=True)`; the same inputs give identical frames (REPLAY-04); on a held-out seed a side's score equals its logged evaluation score against the same opponent (REPLAY-05)
433	- **Errors:** as `registry.load_agent` and `registry.make_env`, and `MissingSideError` for a side with no spec, raised before any window opens
434	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.record_replay/ · **Cases:** the scripted agent on Snake seed 42; a saved rung on tank seed 1,000,000; predator generation 10 against prey generation 3 on hunt seed 42
435	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
436	
437	### replay.Playback: type
438	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Playback: frames: tuple[int, ...]; fps: tuple[int, ...]`
439	- **Invariants:** one entry per frame shown, in order: `frames[i]` indexes `Replay.frames` and `fps[i]` is the speed when it was shown, within 1–30; the two have the same length; a pause adds no entry, and a step while paused adds exactly the next index (REPLAY-03)
440	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Playback/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame replay played through at 10 fps; paused after frame 0 then stepped once; sped up to 30 fps after frame 1
441	- **Used by:** cli
442	
443	### replay.show: Replay window
444	`def show(replay: Replay, *, fps: int = 10) -> Playback`
445	- **Invariants:** draws each frame's cells, then its shapes, with the step, each side's score and the caption (REPLAY-02); space pauses, → steps exactly one frame while paused, ↑/↓ change the speed within 1–30 fps (REPLAY-03); stays on the last frame until closed, then returns what it showed; reads keys only from the pygame event queue, so a test drives it headless with SDL's dummy video driver and posted key events
446	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `fps` is outside 1–30
447	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.show/ · **Cases:** a 3-frame corridor replay shown headless; a 3-frame tank replay shown headless; a 3-frame hunt replay shown headless
448	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05

459	`def build_table(run: Path, *, games: int = 20) -> CrossTable`
460	- **Invariants:** reads the run's record, loads every saved generation with `registry.load_agent(f"{run}@{n}", env, side)`, plays each row generation against each column generation on the first `games` held-out seeds with `play_game(..., explore=False)` (CROSS-01), and writes `run/crossplay.json`; never trains (CROSS-02, AD-23)

484	## study
485	
486	### study.Variant: type
487	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Variant: agents: dict[str, AgentSpec]; selfplay: SelfPlayConfig | None = None; generations: int = 0`
488	- **Invariants:** one `AgentSpec` per side of the plan's environment; fills `RunConfig`'s fields of the same names
489	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.Variant/ · **Cases:** Snake's `no-memory`; the tank's `ppo`; the hunt's `selfplay`
490	- **Used by:** study.StudyPlan
491	
492	### study.StudyPlan: type
493	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyPlan: env: str; full: str; learners: tuple[str, ...]; ablations: tuple[str, ...]; configs: dict[str, Variant]; env_options: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]; sides: tuple[str, ...]; ladder: bool; optional: tuple[str, ...]; crossplay: bool; sections: tuple[str, ...]`
494	- **Invariants:** `configs` has one entry per variant name; `full` is in `learners`, the agents compared with both baselines, in rung order; `ladder` compares each learner with the one before; `ablations` are compared with `full`; `optional` learners may be skipped (SELF-06); `crossplay` builds each run's table and compares each side's last generation with its first; `sections` are the notes headings required (AD-16)
495	- **Invariants (plans):** `STUDIES["snake"]`: `full` / (`full`,) / (`no-memory`, `no-frozen`, `no-decay`), sides (`snake`,), no ladder; `STUDIES["tank"]`: `ppo` / (`reinforce`, `actor-critic`, `ppo`) / (`no-value`, `no-cap`, `no-rescale`), 5 fish, sides (`fish`,), a ladder; `STUDIES["hunt"]`: `selfplay` / (`turns`, `simultaneous`, `selfplay`) / (), both sides rung 3 with 10 generations, 5 prey, sides (`predator`, `prey`), `turns` optional, cross-play, sections "Why it learns", "Why self-play works" and one per rung (AD-24)
496	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyPlan/ · **Cases:** `STUDIES["snake"]`; `STUDIES["tank"]`; `STUDIES["hunt"]`
497	- **Used by:** study.run_study, study.check_notes; PL-04, PL-06
498	
499	### study.Comparison: type
500	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: label: str; verdict: Literal["beats", "does not beat", "better", "hurts learning", "later beats earlier", "no detectable difference", "missing"]; method: str; statistic: float | None; p_value: float | None; p_adjusted: float | None`
501	- **Invariants:** baseline comparisons use "beats", "does not beat" or "missing" (EVAL-03); ladder comparisons "better", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (PG-10); ablation comparisons "hurts learning", "no detectable difference" or "missing" (ABL-04); generation comparisons "later beats earlier" or "no detectable difference" (CROSS-03); "missing" has no statistics; in a two-sided environment `label` names the side
502	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.Comparison/ · **Cases:** the agent beats the random baseline; an ablation with no detectable difference; a missing ablation; rung 3 better than rung 2; the self-play prey's later generation beats its earlier
503	- **Used by:** cli
504	
505	### study.compare_scores: One-sided comparison (null-hypothesis-testing)
506	`def compare_scores(full: np.ndarray, other: np.ndarray, *, label: str, n_comparisons: int, alpha: float = 0.05) -> Comparison`
507	- **Invariants:** `full` and `other` are each k × 100 (finished training seed × held-out seed, 1 ≤ k ≤ 5; a baseline is 1 × 100), columns in `EVAL_SEEDS` order; pairs by held-out game on each side's per-game mean over its rows; tests whether `full` is higher, corrected for `n_comparisons`; `method` states the seeds each side used (AD-09); the test is built with the **`null-hypothesis-testing`** skill
508	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the column counts differ, or if either side has no rows or more than 5
509	- **Used by:** study.run_study; PL-04
510	
511	### study.compare_generations: Last generation against first (null-hypothesis-testing)
512	`def compare_generations(last: np.ndarray, first: np.ndarray, *, label: str, n_comparisons: int, alpha: float = 0.05) -> Comparison`
513	- **Invariants:** `last` and `first` are each k × 10 × 20 (complete run seed × opponent generation × held-out game, 1 ≤ k ≤ 5) of one side's scores, rows the same runs in the same order; tests whether `last` is higher, paired by run, opponent generation and game, corrected for `n_comparisons` (CROSS-03, AD-23); `method` states the runs used; the test is built with the **`null-hypothesis-testing`** skill
514	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the shapes differ or k is outside 1–5
515	- **Used by:** study.run_study; PL-04
516	
517	### study.StudyReport: type
518	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class StudyReport: env: str; baselines: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ladder: tuple[Comparison, ...]; ablations: tuple[Comparison, ...]; crossplay: tuple[Comparison, ...]; spreads: dict[str, float]; incomplete: tuple[str, ...]; report: Path; charts: tuple[Path, ...]; tables: tuple[Path, ...]`
519	- **Invariants:** 2 baseline comparisons per learner and side, one ladder comparison per rung above the first in a ladder, 3 ablation comparisons, 2 cross-play comparisons per learner (Snake: 2, 0, 3, 0; tank: 6, 2, 3, 0; hunt: 12, 0, 0, 6, or 8, 0, 0, 4 without `turns`); `spreads` is each learner's (and side's) standard deviation of its per-seed mean final score (PG-10); `incomplete` names runs left out (EVAL and CROSS edge cases); a missed bar the spec calls a finding is stated as one and the study still succeeds (ABL-05, PG-11, SELF-08); `charts` is `curves.png` plus `ladder.png` in a ladder, or `curves-<side>.png` per side (CURVE-07); `tables` is one `crossplay-<rung>.md` per rung with runs, each a `TablePage.path`
520	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.StudyReport/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake study; a study where all 3 ablations show no detectable difference; a study with a failed ablation run; a tank study where rung 3 shows no detectable difference; a hunt study without `turns` where the simultaneous predator shows no detectable difference
521	- **Used by:** cli
522	
523	### study.run_study: The full study
524	`def run_study(env_name: str = "snake", seeds: Sequence[int] = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5), *, n_games: int = 1_000, workers: int | None = None, skip: Sequence[str] = (), root: Path = Path("runs")) -> StudyReport`
525	- **Invariants:** trains every variant of `STUDIES[env_name]` but the `skip`ped optional ones, for each seed in parallel processes, on `registry.eval_schedule`'s cadence; scores the baselines on `EVAL_SEEDS`; builds each finished run's cross-play table in the same pool when the plan has cross-play; compares on each run's `final` and tables, correcting within each family (AD-16); a skipped learner leaves every family and table (SELF-06); writes `root/<env>/study/report.md`, its charts and tables; runs unattended (QR-02)
526	- **Errors:** a failed run is reported as missing or incomplete, never as a difference: a learner's unfinished runs are left out of its baseline and cross-play comparisons, which use its finished ones; an ablation, or a rung in a ladder comparison, with any unfinished run is `missing` (AD-09); `ValueError` naming the learner if `skip` names one that isn't optional; `RuntimeError` naming the runs only if `full` has no finished run
527	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.run_study/ · **Cases:** a 2-seed, 100-game corridor study with one ablation run failing; the same study with one full-agent run failing; a 2-seed, 20-game line study of the 3 rungs; a 2-seed, 20-game chase study of the 3 self-play rungs with `turns` skipped
528	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04
529	
530	### study.NotesCheck: type
531	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class NotesCheck: page: Path; exists: bool; missing_sections: tuple[str, ...]; empty_sections: tuple[str, ...]; broken_links: tuple[str, ...]; ok: bool`
532	- **Invariants:** required sections are the plan's `sections`, so a page checked before any study still gets a section check: "Why it learns" (EXPL-01), one per ablation under its variant name (EXPL-02), one per ladder rung after the first under its rung name (EXPL-04), and the hunt's "Why self-play works" and one per rung (EXPL-05), an optional rung's only once it has runs; `broken_links` names each of `runs/<env>/study/report.md`, its charts and its tables that the page doesn't link or whose link doesn't resolve from the page's folder (EXPL-03); when `exists` is false every required section is missing; `ok` is true exactly when the page exists and all three tuples are empty
533	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.NotesCheck/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page; a missing page; a page with an empty "Why it learns" section and no chart link; a tank page missing its `ppo` section; a hunt page missing its "Why self-play works" section and a cross-play table link
534	- **Used by:** cli; PL-06
535	
536	### study.check_notes: Check the learner's notes page
537	`def check_notes(env_name: str, *, notes_root: Path, runs_root: Path) -> NotesCheck`
538	- **Invariants:** reads `notes_root/<env>.md` and `runs_root/<env>/study/report.md` only; a section is a Markdown heading of any level, matched case-insensitively, and is empty when no non-blank text comes before the next heading of the same or a higher level; never writes, creates or changes a file, so the page is byte-identical afterwards (AD-10)
539	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming the studied environments for an environment with no `StudyPlan`; none for a missing page or a missing report, which show up in the result
540	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/study.check_notes/ · **Cases:** a complete Snake page beside a study folder; no page; a page whose chart link points to a missing file; a complete tank page; a complete hunt page
541	- **Used by:** cli; PL-06
